- **Ship Orchestrator**: Drives end-to-end implementation of an Azure DevOps work item using Claude Code. Fetches work item details (title, description, acceptance criteria), creates a git branch, explores the codebase, plans the implementation, writes code, runs tests, opens a PR linked to the work item, and updates the work item state to Resolved -- all in a single resumable workflow. Use this agent when the user says "ship work item", "ship devops task", "implement work item {id}", "build work item {id}", "work on devops item {id}", or "ship #{id}". &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to implement an Azure DevOps work i… ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/azure-devops-orchestrator/agents/ship-orchestrator.md) · ★19)
- **Ship Orchestrator**: Drives end-to-end implementation of a Microsoft Planner task using Claude Code. Fetches task details, creates a git branch, explores the codebase, plans the implementation, writes code, runs tests, opens a PR, and marks the Planner task Done — all in a single resumable workflow. Use this agent when the user says "ship planner task", "implement this planner task", "build what's in planner task", "ship {taskId}", or "work on planner task {taskId}". &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to implement a Planner task as code user: "Ship the planner task abc123" assistant: "I'll use the ship-orchestrator ag… ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/planner-orchestrator/agents/ship-orchestrator.md) · ★19)
- **ship-a-workbench-pack**: Take-home. Your team has the workbench surfaces as loose scripts, a shared document and some memory. Package them into one directory that a new repository can install with one command, and defend what you leave out. Bring the layout, the installer, the versioning rule and the uninstall rule. We grade the second install as much as the first. ([rohitg00/ai-engineering-from-scratch](https://github.com/rohitg00/ai-engineering-from-scratch/blob/HEAD/interviews/questions/agents/ship-a-workbench-pack.md) · ★80k · 4 copies)
- **ship-a-workbench-pack**: 带回作业（Take-home）。你的团队目前将工作台各个表面零散地实现为各自独立的脚本、一份共享文档以及部分口头记忆。请将它们打包为一个统一的目录，使得任何新代码库都能通过单条命令完成安装，并为你主动排除的内容辩护。提交目录结构、安装程序、版本控制规则以及卸载规则。我们对第二次重复执行安装的检验与第一次同样严格。 ([fancyboi999/ai-engineering-from-scratch-zh](https://github.com/fancyboi999/ai-engineering-from-scratch-zh/blob/HEAD/interviews/questions/agents/ship-a-workbench-pack.md) · ★1.4k)
- **ship-analyst**: Autonomous requirement-analysis agent for the /ship SOP. Spawned when the coding agent hits an unexpected branch or unplanned decision point. - **ship-and-babysit**: Commit local changes, push the branch to the user's fork, open or reuse a PR against tinyhumansai/openhuman:main, then babysit CI and CodeRabbit feedback until the PR is green and clean. - **ship-auditor**: Audits "done" claims against actual visible output. Use proactively when an agent or human says "shipped", "deployed", "verified", "done", or claims a user can now see something. - **ship-check**: Pre-ship product QA for a user journey. Thinks like a product manager who has shipped and been burned, not like a code reviewer or a linter. - **ship-check**: Runs a /ship plan's dev check against a deployed environment (bypassPermissions). Dispatch only when a skill names it explicitly (e.g. /ship); never pick it on your own. ([Wedvich/dotfiles](https://github.com/Wedvich/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ship-check.md))
- **ship-checker**: Use this agent before shipping to run a multi-dimensional quality check. - **ship-devops-reviewer**: Expert DevOps/release reviewer for the ship pipeline's QA and prod-prep phases. - **ship-gate-judge**: LLM-as-judge for ship pipeline gates. Scores a phase's artifact against a fixed rubric (0-100) and returns pass/fail vs a threshold, with concrete reasons. - **ship-gates**: What each gate in the ship profile's `## Local gate` runs and why. ([Gharib89/skills](https://github.com/Gharib89/skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ship-gates.md))
- **ship-guard**: Pre-commit safety reviewer for the two-repo DevOpsWorker workspace. Reads the staged diff and answers what the commit hook can't cheaply judge: (1) are these files in the RIGHT repo — generic pipeline code to the public core, site-specific agents/deploy/registries/docs to the private overlay? (2) does the diff leak customer/tenant names, internal repo URLs or paths, internal tool names, environment IDs, or secrets? Use before committing, especially to the public core. - **ship-implementation-lead**: Implementation lead for accepted specs. Writes the smallest correct patch, honors scope lock/non-goals, edits code/docs, runs focused validation, and stops. - **ship-lookout**: Lightweight read-only Ship lookout for quick checks and investigations. - **ship-mate-implement**: Developer for the ShipMate pipeline. Use when /ship reaches the implement stage after plan approval or a fix loop. &lt;example&gt; user: "/ship resume" assistant: "Plan approved. - **ship-mate-orchestrate**: Product Orchestrator for the ShipMate pipeline. Use when /ship reaches the orchestrate stage for a task. &lt;example&gt; user: "/ship stories/password-reset.md" assistant: "Scan done. Delegating the first task to the ship-mate-orchestrate agent." &lt;/example&gt; ([shotcowboystyle/ag-skills](https://github.com/shotcowboystyle/ag-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship-mate/agents/orchestrate.md))
- **ship-mate-playwright**: Playwright E2E tester for the ShipMate pipeline. Writes page-object Playwright tests for the task's browser-visible acceptance criteria, runs them with Playwright's webServer managing the dev server, and returns pass/fail results. Use when /ship reaches the playwright stage for a FRONTEND or FULLSTACK task after QA passes. &lt;example&gt; user: "/ship resume" assistant: "QA passed on a FRONTEND task. Delegating to the ship-mate-playwright agent." &lt;/example&gt; ([shotcowboystyle/ag-skills](https://github.com/shotcowboystyle/ag-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship-mate/agents/playwright.md))
- **ship-mate-qa**: QA engineer for the ShipMate pipeline. Tests every acceptance criterion, edge case and error state from orchestrator-output.md, runs the existing test suite for regressions, and returns a QA report with bug triage. Use when /ship reaches the qa stage after review approval. Does not edit code. &lt;example&gt; user: "/ship resume" assistant: "Review approved. Delegating to the ship-mate-qa agent." &lt;/example&gt; ([shotcowboystyle/ag-skills](https://github.com/shotcowboystyle/ag-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship-mate/agents/qa.md))
- **ship-mate-review**: PR reviewer for the ShipMate pipeline. Reviews the task's changes against the recorded base commit with a three-tier taxonomy (🔴 Critical / 🟡 Should Fix / 💡 Consider) and security checklist, and returns the review report. Use when /ship reaches the review stage after implementation. Read-only. &lt;example&gt; user: "/ship resume" assistant: "Implementation finished. Delegating to the ship-mate-review agent with the task base commit." &lt;/example&gt; ([shotcowboystyle/ag-skills](https://github.com/shotcowboystyle/ag-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship-mate/agents/review.md))
- **ship-mate__architect**: Architect agent. Reads orchestrator-output.md, AGENTS.md, and project-doc.md to produce a numbered step-by-step implementation plan. Pauses for human approval before implementation begins. ([KENo548/jarvis](https://github.com/KENo548/jarvis/blob/HEAD/_opencode/agents/ship-mate__architect.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-mate__implement**: Developer agent. Implements the architect plan step-by-step, guided by AGENTS.md guardrails. Writes tests, leaves no TODOs, and flags plan deviations rather than silently skipping them. ([Alan-uai/PixelFandom](https://github.com/Alan-uai/PixelFandom/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ship-mate__implement.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-mate__orchestrate**: Product Orchestrator agent. Reads the active story file, asks clarifying product questions one at a time, confirms task type (FRONTEND/BACKEND), and produces a complete unambiguous spec in orchestrator-output.md. ([Alan-uai/PixelFandom](https://github.com/Alan-uai/PixelFandom/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ship-mate__orchestrate.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-mate__playwright**: Playwright testing agent. Only runs for FRONTEND tasks. Verifies acceptance criteria in a real browser using the page object pattern. Passes on success, routes back to developer on failure. ([KENo548/jarvis](https://github.com/KENo548/jarvis/blob/HEAD/_opencode/agents/ship-mate__playwright.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-mate__qa**: QA Agent. Tests all acceptance criteria and edge cases from orchestrator-output.md. Generates a structured qa-report.md with pass/fail per criterion and bug triage. Loops back to implement if bugs found (max 2 iterations). ([KENo548/jarvis](https://github.com/KENo548/jarvis/blob/HEAD/_opencode/agents/ship-mate__qa.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-mate__review**: PR Reviewer agent. Reviews implemented code using a 3-tier taxonomy (🔴 Critical / 🟡 Should Fix / 💡 Consider). Auto-resolves minor issues, pauses on critical ones. Applies security guardrails. Outputs review-report.md. ([KENo548/jarvis](https://github.com/KENo548/jarvis/blob/HEAD/_opencode/agents/ship-mate__review.md) · 2 copies)
- **ship-monitor**: &gt; ([Ritheswara/Vox-Agent](https://github.com/Ritheswara/Vox-Agent/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/mutagent-ship/assets/agents/ship-monitor.md) · 4 copies)
- **ship-occams-principles**: Final simplicity and engineering-principles reviewer for implementation patches. Cuts scope creep, speculative abstractions, dependency bloat, noisy docs/tests, and review friction. Use before final /ship validation or PR polish. ([Zhachory1/mewritecode](https://github.com/Zhachory1/mewritecode/blob/HEAD/packages/coding-agent/agents/ship-occams-principles.md) · ★2)
- **ship-operator**: Executes all 5 gated phases of the release pipeline sequentially, enforcing gate conditions before advancing. ([catlog22/Claude-Code-Workflow](https://github.com/catlog22/Claude-Code-Workflow/blob/HEAD/.codex/skills/ship/agents/ship-operator.md))
- **ship-ops**: Executes git tag, PR merge, and push operations. Mechanical shipping — no judgment calls, no source code modifications. ([7alexhale5-rgb/shiploop](https://github.com/7alexhale5-rgb/shiploop/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-ops.md))
- **ship-orchestrator**: ship 워크플로우를 제어하는 오케스트레이터. /ship 스킬에서 호출된다. 직접 코드를 작성하지 않고, 전문 에이전트에게 위임하여 구현 → 테스트 → PR → 리뷰 → 머지 → 학습을 완수한다. ([nanana3679/hada-reboot](https://github.com/nanana3679/hada-reboot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ship-orchestrator.md))
- **ship-oss-scout**: Reuse scout for the ship research phase. Given a project idea/positioning, scans for existing open-source projects, libraries, and tools that already solve the problem (or big parts of it), reports their pros/cons, and returns a build-vs-adopt-vs-fork verdict so the owner doesn't reinvent the wheel. Use early in research, before building anything. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-oss-scout.md) · ★1)
- **ship-phase-runner**: Runs ONE ship pipeline phase in an isolated, fresh context from a context pack — used for the linear phases (build, fix, prod-prep, provision, deploy, monitor). Not for QA (phase 4), which the orchestrator fans out into the expert panel itself. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-phase-runner.md) · ★1)
- **ship-pilot**: Chrome-enabled Ship crew for browser interaction watches. Use when the Mate dispatches work requiring browser automation (screenshots, UI verification, form testing). - **ship-plan-critic**: Critique indépendant d'un plan d'implémentation, à la manière ponytail — ce qu'on peut retirer, ce que l'existant fait déjà, ce qui manque (sécurité, obligations du repo). Rend PASS ou REVISE avec des points bloquants. - **ship-scan**: You assemble evidence; you decide nothing. Your job is to make that decision easy to take on evidence — or to report, typed, that there is nothing to decid ([watt-mind/factory](https://github.com/watt-mind/factory/blob/HEAD/event-runtime/agents/ship-scan.md) · ★20)
- **ship-scanner**: Scans changed files for convention violations — TODOs, scaffolding remnants, AI attribution, missing exports, language mixing, console.log remnants, hardcoded dates, JSX attribute misuse, hardcoded Tailwind colors, and pure black/white usage. Ray's ruling (2026-10-04) is an **executing** scheduler, the only thing that decides when ship/land runs. Scratch data from this run: `/tmp/shipsched/` (`df_prs.json`, `kb_prs.json`, `df_runs.json`, `logs.txt`, `humfiles.jsonl`). It is machine-local and not durable. Every number below names the command that produced it. - **ship-scout**: Read-only recon agent for /ship runs. Use to map a codebase area fast — relevant files, existing utilities to reuse, test setup, tooling commands, risks. - **ship-scout**: Inventorie ce qui existe déjà dans le repo courant pour un besoin donné (composants, modules, routes, services, schéma, helpers, doc) avant qu'un plan ne soit écrit. Lecture seule. À lancer en début de /ship, avec le besoin en deux phrases et le chemin du profil de repo (.ship/repo.md). Rend PASS ou FIX avec des findings prouvés. Lecture seule. À lancer dans /ship en parallèle de la revue produit et de la code-review, avec la branche, .ship/&lt;slug&gt;/ et le profil de repo. ([MielPopsssssss/claude-setup](https://github.com/MielPopsssssss/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/bundle/agents/ship-security.md))

- **ship-security-reviewer**: Expert application-security reviewer for the ship pipeline's QA phase. Audits authn/authz, injection, SSRF, secret handling, the untrusted-data boundary, and webhook/billing integrity. Use during phase-4 expert QA before any launch, especially for public SaaS and anything handling money or PII. Use during phase-4 expert QA of any content site or SaaS with a marketing surface. ([vadimsv1/ship-plugin](https://github.com/vadimsv1/ship-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/ship-seo-expert.md) · ★1)
- **ship-seo-strategist**: Go-to-market / SEO strategist for the ship research phase (and revisited in monitor). Researches keyword demand, competitor SERP landscape, audience channels, and a distribution + content/programmatic-SEO plan for how to actually get the project found and adopted. Distinct from ship-seo-expert, which audits on-page SEO of built pages — this one plans promotion. Use for anything with a marketing surface. ([cgaravitoq/motion-shorts](https://github.com/cgaravitoq/motion-shorts/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/short-qa.md) · ★2)

([cgaravitoq/motion-shorts](https://github.com/cgaravitoq/motion-shorts/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/short-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **short-seller**: Short-seller persona for a templeton hunt. Researches one ticker via web search, builds the strongest possible permanent-impairment case against it, then reports where it honestly lands as a structured TEMPORARY/PERMANENT verdict with cited evidence. Use when stress-testing the bull case on a beaten-down stock, or as the bear seat on the templeton panel. ([ccomkhj/stock_interogation](https://github.com/ccomkhj/stock_interogation/blob/HEAD/agents/short-seller.md))
- **short-story-forge-critic**: 短篇小说反套路审查官。严苛审查正文中的套路、AI痕迹、逻辑漏洞，输出问题清单。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to review story text. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-critic.md))
- **short-story-forge-emotion**: 短篇小说情绪强化师。强化文本中的情绪张力、共鸣点和代入感，让读者真正被触动。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to enhance emotional impact. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-emotion.md))

- **short-story-forge-memory**: 短篇小说记忆系统。QA通过后记录本篇创作元数据，积累风格经验。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to update story memory. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-memory.md))

- **short-story-forge-qa**: 短篇小说质量审查员。对最终正文执行硬性质量检查：禁词扫描、字数统计、标点检查、完整性验证。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator for final quality check. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-qa.md))
- **short-story-forge-styler**: 短篇小说文风打磨师。深度去AI化，优化节奏感、句式变化、口语化程度，让文字读起来像真人作者。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to polish writing style. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-styler.md))
- **short-story-forge-writer**: 短篇小说正文写手。根据Planner的结构计划写8000-12000字中文短篇正文。Use when coordinated by short-story-forge coordinator to write story content. ([LINHANXIN/duanpian](https://github.com/LINHANXIN/duanpian/blob/HEAD/agents/short-story-forge-writer.md))

Groups beats into 10 second generations, writes one CINEDANCE prompt per shot with each character's Acting System scene paragraph and locked voice prompt, prices drafts and finals, and writes work/&lt;slug&gt;/shots.json. Spends nothing. Also rewrites a single shot after Caleb marks a draft for a re-roll. ([verillumai/the-ninth-room](https://github.com/verillumai/the-ninth-room/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shot-director.md) · ★1)
- **shot-finder**: Finds the needed shot in the video bank from a description in the script. Use when, for a script beat, you need to find which file and from which second to take a piece — "hands in foam, close-up", "a stain on white fabric", "a bottle in a hand". Looks at the frames itself and returns only timecodes, without clogging the main context with pictures. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shot-finder.md) · ★1)
- **shot-finder**: Ищет нужный кадр в банке видео по описанию из сценария. Использовать, когда для бита сценария нужно найти, из какого файла и с какой секунды брать кусок — «руки в пене крупно», «пятно на белой ткани», «бутылка в руке». Смотрит кадры сам и возвращает только таймкоды, не засоряя основной контекст картинками. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/docs/ru/agents/shot-finder.md) · ★1)
- **shot-fixer**: Fixes exactly the beats Caleb flagged in the Edit Room — reads work/&lt;slug&gt;/review.json, patches only those beats' trims/cuts/b-roll/cards/captions, re-proxies them, and marks them reworked. Never touches an approved beat. Use whenever review.json has flagged entries. ([verillumai/the-ninth-room](https://github.com/verillumai/the-ninth-room/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shot-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **shot-gate-reviewer**: Read-only Per-Shot Post-Media Gate reviewer. After a Shot's exact MP4 lands, calls the project video-analysis MCP and judges it item by item against the sealed intent, required checks and prior accepted state, returning PASS/FAIL/NOT_EVALUATED evidence for the Parent to adjudicate. Use for 单镜成片检查、逐镜 Gate; not for aesthetic approval or repairs. ([Reggie-sun/AI-VIDEO](https://github.com/Reggie-sun/AI-VIDEO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shot-gate-reviewer.md))
- **shot-judge**: 시안의 화면·설명·흐름을 실물로 검수한다. editorial-concept는 전체 시청 경험과 개념 표현을, script-faithful는 제공 대본에 대한 비트별 A/B 적합성을 판정한다. ([azuri-pixel/shortform-news-liveaction](https://github.com/azuri-pixel/shortform-news-liveaction/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/shot-judge.md))
- **shot-reviewer**: Judges rendered frames or contact sheets of a VJ scene against a written rubric and returns one JSON verdict per shot. Use it for every visual check so images never enter the builder's context. Give it the rubric file, the image paths (at most 36) and the round number. ([abossard/bossi3dj](https://github.com/abossard/bossi3dj/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shot-reviewer.md))
- **shot-taker**: Use when a `web-kit-shot` run (a spec, or `--board` for a mockup board) failed or produced a wrong shot (cut off, illegible, empty), or when a spec for given states of a web page must be written and run by the screenshot rules of browser-checks.md. It operates the command - runs it, repairs the spec or arguments, or reports why it cannot. Read-only toward the app, and it never drives the in-app browser. ([KoroVaik/ClaudeDevKit](https://github.com/KoroVaik/ClaudeDevKit/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-kit/agents/shot-taker.md))
- **shoto-orchestrator**: shoto project orchestrator tuned to this project's stack and conventions. Inherits the full orchestrator:orchestrator contract; never writes files, always delegates to writer subagents. ([shoto290/shoto](https://github.com/shoto290/shoto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/shoto-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **shoudan-crm-updater**: Reads recent customer interactions and pushes the resulting updates into the CRM so opportunity records stay current without manual data entry.
